Quarterback situations

TeamSituation
FLAAaron Philo was named Florida's starter by Jon Sumrall. He has not played since his Oct 10 shoulder injury, and Tramell Jones Jr. started the last game. Philo's arm is the risk here.
ASUJake Fette is modeled as the starter on Kenny Dillingham's words, "The plan is probably Jake." Mikey Keene (knee or ankle, reports differ) is a game-time decision and Cutter Boley was benched. Every ASU passing number is low confidence, and Fette has thrown only 8 passes.
OSULuke Fahey is the reported starter with Julian Sayin out, but Ryan Day has not named one and says both Fahey and Tavien St. Clair could play. He has very few career attempts, so OSU passing numbers are low confidence.
NAVYJackson Gutierrez runs the triple option (150 carries, 869 yards per previews). Usage data alone read him as a running back, so he was set as QB by hand.
NEVLuke Duncan started the latest game, Nevada's third starter this year. Carter Jones is listed questionable with a stale note. Low confidence.
UCLATy Dieffenbach has started since Nico Iamaleava went out with a leg injury. He holds 37% of the team's attempts, so the model discounts his efficiency.
MSSTParker Puckett has started the last two games. Kamario Taylor is listed questionable, and the two have split the job.

Starters on other teams come from the latest game played, not a confirmed announcement.

Player probabilities are close to honest. Holdout buckets landed within about 3 points of actual hit rates, except the top two, which ran 4 points high. Those high numbers now take a haircut, fit on the same holdout, so that correction has not been tested on new games.

Game lines show no edge. Spread MAE was 11.84 points for the close vs 13.06 for our line. Total MAE was 12.73 vs 13.26, across 291 games. The best-fit weight on our line was negative, so every game side here is a pass.

Prop value is unknown. No college prop lines were available, so no prop EV is claimed. The small sample covers six weeks of one season.

Data gaps

  • Prop lines and prices: none found. Each game gives fair lines and limits instead. Moneyline, spread and total prices are real DraftKings numbers via ESPN.
  • Scheme: no man/zone, coverage shell, blitz or pressure rate, routes, alignment or snap counts exist in free college data. Throw location appears only in games with charted text. Nothing here is inferred from a coach's reputation.
  • Positions: roles come from usage, so tight ends show as WR.
  • Injuries: college has no required injury report. Covers is unofficial, and P(plays) values are assumptions. In the backtest 35% of eligible players did not log a stat, so absences are a large risk.
  • Sample: 2026 only for market-tied testing, because ESPN holds few 2025 lines.

Sources: ESPN, NWS, Covers injuries, Florida QB, Ohio State QB, Sayin injury, Arizona State QB, Navy preview.

These are estimates, and no selection is guaranteed. After the games, run review_cfb.py to score every forecast (Phase 15).

GGame picks

Call: pass on game lines. The sim sits on the market, and our own line lost to it in the backtest (margin MAE 13.06 vs 11.84), so no side is recommended. Best listed EV is Under 50.5 at +100 (+3.2%), which is noise.

HConservative, balanced, aggressive

Conservative: Jeremiah Smith 20+ receiving yds (87%) + Jayden Maiava 150+ passing yds (83%). Joint estimate 72.4%, fair -263. These legs are approximately independent: sim joint 75.7% vs 75.4% if independent. Not guaranteed.

Balanced: King Miller 20+ rushing yds (79%) + Trent Mosley 20+ receiving yds (78%) + Jayden Maiava 175+ passing yds (77%). Joint estimate 51.2%, fair -105. These legs are positively correlated: sim joint 52.8% vs 48.9% if independent. Not guaranteed.
